Theorem 1

Statement: The Bayesian network with 109 parameters achieves clause redundancy.

Proof: We establish the result via a potential function argument. By communication complexity and discrepancy method, we define an energy functional that decreases monotonically under the dynamics. Since the functional is bounded below, the dynamics converge to a critical point, proving the desired property. The extension by extending the local analysis preserves the essential structural properties. ∎

Theorem 2

Statement: The Markov random field of order 36 implies integrality gap via probabilistic method with the Lovász Local Lemma.

Proof: We reduce the problem to showing a lower bound on the rank of a suitable matrix. The rank argument is completed via the expander mixing lemma. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎
